drills-area-and-volume-06 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Area and volume · difficulty 3
A rectangular tank $50$ cm by $40$ cm is filled with water to a depth of $30$ cm. How many litres of water does it hold? ($1$ litre $= 1{,}000$ cm$^3$)

Type-in answer: 60

Answer 60. $50 \times 40 \times 30 = 60{,}000$ cm$^3 = 60$ litres.

Lines, angles and triangles · 6 questions

drills-lines-angles-and-triangles-01 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Lines, angles and triangles · difficulty 1
Two angles on a straight line measure $70^\circ$ and $x^\circ$. What is $x$?
  1. $20$
  2. $90$
  3. $110$
  4. $290$
Answer C. Angles on a straight line add to $180^\circ$: $x = 110$.
drills-lines-angles-and-triangles-02 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Lines, angles and triangles · difficulty 1
In an equilateral triangle, what is the measure of each angle?
  1. $60^\circ$
  2. $45^\circ$
  3. $90^\circ$
  4. $120^\circ$
Answer A. The three equal angles share $180^\circ$: $60^\circ$ each.
drills-lines-angles-and-triangles-03 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Lines, angles and triangles · difficulty 2
Two parallel lines are cut by a transversal. One angle measures $65^\circ$. Its corresponding angle measures $x^\circ$. What is $x$?

Type-in answer: 65

Answer 65. Corresponding angles on parallel lines are equal: $65$.
drills-lines-angles-and-triangles-04 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Lines, angles and triangles · difficulty 2
Triangles $ABC$ and $DEF$ are similar with $AB = 4$ and $DE = 10$. If $AC = 6$, what is $DF$?
  1. $12$
  2. $15$
  3. $16$
  4. $24$
Answer B. Scale factor $= \frac{10}{4} = 2.5$, so $DF = 6 \times 2.5 = 15$.
drills-lines-angles-and-triangles-05 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Lines, angles and triangles · difficulty 3
In triangle $XYZ$, angle $X$ is twice angle $Y$, and angle $Z$ is $30^\circ$. What is angle $X$?
  1. $50^\circ$
  2. $75^\circ$
  3. $120^\circ$
  4. $100^\circ$
Answer D. $2y + y + 30 = 180$, so $y = 50$ and angle $X = 100^\circ$.
drills-lines-angles-and-triangles-06 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Lines, angles and triangles · difficulty 3
An exterior angle of a triangle measures $130^\circ$. One of the two opposite interior angles measures $55^\circ$. What is the other, in degrees?

Type-in answer: 75

Answer 75. An exterior angle equals the sum of the two opposite interior angles: $130 - 55 = 75$.

Right triangles and trigonometry · 6 questions

drills-right-triangles-and-trigonometry-01 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Right triangles and trigonometry · difficulty 1
A right triangle has legs $5$ and $12$. What is the length of the hypotenuse?

Type-in answer: 13

Answer 13. $\sqrt{25 + 144} = \sqrt{169} = 13$.
drills-right-triangles-and-trigonometry-02 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Right triangles and trigonometry · difficulty 1
In a right triangle, the side opposite angle $A$ is $3$ and the hypotenuse is $5$. What is $\sin A$?
  1. $\dfrac{3}{4}$
  2. $\dfrac{4}{5}$
  3. $\dfrac{3}{5}$
  4. $\dfrac{5}{3}$
Answer C. $\sin A = \frac{\text{opposite}}{\text{hypotenuse}} = \frac{3}{5}$.
drills-right-triangles-and-trigonometry-03 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Right triangles and trigonometry · difficulty 2
A $45^\circ$-$45^\circ$-$90^\circ$ triangle has legs of length $7$. What is the hypotenuse?
  1. $7$
  2. $7\sqrt{2}$
  3. $7\sqrt{3}$
  4. $14$
Answer B. In a $45$-$45$-$90$ triangle the hypotenuse is $\sqrt{2}$ times a leg.
drills-right-triangles-and-trigonometry-04 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Right triangles and trigonometry · difficulty 2
A $30^\circ$-$60^\circ$-$90^\circ$ triangle has a hypotenuse of $10$. What is the length of the side opposite the $30^\circ$ angle?
  1. $20$
  2. $5\sqrt{3}$
  3. $10\sqrt{3}$
  4. $5$
Answer D. The side opposite $30^\circ$ is half the hypotenuse: $5$.
drills-right-triangles-and-trigonometry-05 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Right triangles and trigonometry · difficulty 3
If $\sin x^\circ = \cos 25^\circ$ and $0 < x < 90$, what is $x$?
  1. $25$
  2. $55$
  3. $65$
  4. $75$
Answer C. $\sin x^\circ = \cos(90 - x)^\circ$, so $90 - x = 25$ and $x = 65$.
drills-right-triangles-and-trigonometry-06 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Right triangles and trigonometry · difficulty 3
A ramp rises $1.5$ m over a horizontal distance of $2$ m. How long is the ramp, in metres?

Type-in answer: 2.5

Answer 2.5. $\sqrt{1.5^2 + 2^2} = \sqrt{6.25} = 2.5$.

Circles · 6 questions

drills-circles-01 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Circles · difficulty 1
What is the circumference of a circle with radius $5$?
  1. $5\pi$
  2. $25\pi$
  3. $10\pi$
  4. $50\pi$
Answer C. $C = 2\pi r = 10\pi$.
drills-circles-02 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Circles · difficulty 1
What is the area of a circle with radius $6$?
  1. $6\pi$
  2. $36\pi$
  3. $12\pi$
  4. $72\pi$
Answer B. $A = \pi r^2 = 36\pi$.
drills-circles-03 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Circles · difficulty 2
A circle has equation $(x - 3)^2 + (y + 2)^2 = 49$. What is its radius?
  1. $3$
  2. $7$
  3. $24.5$
  4. $49$
Answer B. The right side is $r^2 = 49$, so $r = 7$.
drills-circles-04 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Circles · difficulty 2
A central angle of $90^\circ$ cuts off an arc in a circle of radius $8$. The arc length is $k\pi$. What is $k$?

Type-in answer: 4

Answer 4. $\frac{90}{360} \times 2\pi(8) = 4\pi$.
drills-circles-05 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Circles · difficulty 3
What is the centre of the circle $x^2 + y^2 - 10x + 4y = 7$?
  1. $(10, -4)$
  2. $(-5, 2)$
  3. $(5, -2)$
  4. $(-10, 4)$
Answer C. Complete the square: $(x - 5)^2 + (y + 2)^2 = 36$, so the centre is $(5, -2)$.
drills-circles-06 · Geometry and Trigonometry · Circles · difficulty 3
A sector of a circle of radius $9$ has a central angle of $40^\circ$. Its area is $k\pi$. What is $k$?

Type-in answer: 9

Answer 9. $\frac{40}{360} \times \pi(81) = 9\pi$.
